CLC chloride channels in plants: Plant CLC channels (like AtCLC-c in Arabidopsis) are known to be located in the thylakoid membrane and are voltage-dependent Cl- channels. They help partition the proton-motive force between Δψ (membrane potential) and ΔpH (proton gradient). When Cl- flows into the lumen, it compensates for some of the charge movement, allowing more ΔpH to build up relative to Δψ.

- They are voltage-gated
